`LocalDayClockTest` checks day identity follows the supplied local timezone across UTC midnight and changes at local midnight. `MercySystemTest` covers heart cap, near-miss/kindness chain behavior, hit reset, integer saturation, final-representable increment, and complete run-local reset. `MultipartBirdSweepIntegrationTest` drives real Tit and Chickadee groups at max scroll with synthesized player motion where both endpoints miss but intervening per-bird swept geometry overlaps; it asserts HIT wins and no mercy/pass reward leaks. `NonTerminalCollisionFeedbackPresenterIntegrationTest` checks Android stumble/mercy presenters emit exactly the canonical authored bubble/flavor pair and lifetimes once, and invalid anchors enqueue no feedback. No test execution.

`OpeningReadabilityGuideTest` checks the authored opening progression (first Duck cue, tap/hold cues, route-facing state, guided-window end), spawn lock/interval/pool pacing, safe invalid-time fallback, and conservative fallback cadence for malformed intervals. `OpeningSeededGuidanceIntegrationTest` connects `EntityManager.seedOpeningSequence` to the first tutorial cue, validating Duck entity, matching authored line, and incomplete Duck action chip. `OwlDepartureIntegrationTest` parallels the Eagle integration for warned Owl attacks: visible vertical escape earns exactly one deferred clean pass, final fast segment cannot tunnel into that reward, Bloom converts exclusively, ordinary sleeping Owl uses its x-plane pass, and state reset/terminal precedence prevent invalid credit. Tests were not run.

`PacifistPresentationTest` checks mercy-miss copy progression, reward copy by kind/biome, tier-specific afterglow, cumulative route world labels, saturation-safe route counts, and corrupted negative counts contributing nothing. `PacifistTrackerTest` covers biome-friendship eligibility/blocked-after-hit, escalating route tiers, spare/clean streak rewards, FIFO preservation when multiple reward classes coincide, reset of counters/queue/biome/tier state, integer saturation, and a bounded queue that retains at most 16 rewards. Tests unexecuted.

`PackedArgbTest` pins exact opaque RGB/ARGB bit layout and channel bounds and confirms biome/mood catalogues initialize without Android framework color calls. `ParallaxBackgroundTest` verifies biome-specific art profile mappings and day/night/Bloom density, sway, gust, glow, mist, leaf and alpha relationships within clamps. `ParallaxBloomAdmissionIntegrationTest` checks finite unit clamping and nonfinite activation/afterglow fail-closed values. `ParallaxFrameInputIntegrationTest` snapshots coordinator accumulators/layer positions to prove malformed frames are complete no-ops, huge finite input is consistently bounded through coordinator and layers, and valid frames repair previously nonfinite accumulators. `ParallaxLayerTest` checks bitmap seam wrapping, huge modular movement, invalid-input no-op behavior, safe external x assignment, draw safety, and invalid speed fractions. `PersistentMemoryManagerTest` checks repeated-history snapshot unlocks/featured selection, threshold clamping that never invents history, minimum one-event admission from negative thresholds, and deterministic tie ordering by catalogue. No tests run.

`PostRunReflectionPlannerTest` checks REST/Garden reflection prioritization (route, world, reflection) and duplicate suppression when sanctuary/recovery/carry-home lines overlap. `RandomEncounterFamilyLeadIntegrationTest` constructs every ordinary eligible entity family across four screen heights at maximum scroll speed, follows production Player-then-EntityManager update order, and verifies creation tick cannot retire/collide entities or consume the generic full-action jump/duck lead budget. This is a broad source-level geometry contract but remains unexecuted.

`RandomEncounterAdmissionIntegrationTest` checks ordinary spawn admission waits for prior encounter resolution and Player recovery from jump/duck/stumble; blocked spacing is retained and permits exactly one fresh encounter after recovery. It also checks compact-screen max-speed spawn lead after same-frame creation/update for random families and Hedgehog, and verifies temporary slow uses undebuffed future speed for the safety lead. Already-resolved and nonpersistent entities are not false blockers. Real `EntityManager`, `Player`, `GameStateManager`, spawn pacing and action feasibility are integrated; tests not run.

`RelationshipArcSystemTest` covers tracked-stage progression, bond formatting/selection, encounter generosity tuning, Dog buddy chance/dialogue/duration, species-specific milestone rewards and wardrobe/homecoming unlocks, and personalized cue/pass/mercy/threat/strained-bond copy for Cat, Fox, Wolf, Dog, Owl and Eagle. It verifies tracked positive histories affect route visitors, warm/trust and strained classifications, with a 40-round concurrent spare/hit stress check that cached stage matches canonical recomputation. Source read only.

`ReadabilityProfileTest` checks density scaling, ground-to-height equivalence, invalid dimension fallback, spawn-gap boundaries/invalid distance behavior, and explicit fairness/readability parameters for flora, trees and hedgehog. `ReducedMotionAccessibilityInvariantTest` proves reduced motion changes only the reduced-motion setting state description; all non-settings semantic trees, actions, and sibling settings data stay identical. `RelationshipCounterBoundsTest` loads `Int.MAX_VALUE` persisted positive/hostile counters and verifies normalization bounds them before scoring/stage/tuning/line selection, including hostile severity without overflow. `RelationshipFamiliarityWarmthIntegrationTest` connects independent persisted pass/spare/kindness/encounter modifiers to distinct personal vs bonded authored thoughts and confirms neutral tone does not receive warmth. Tests unexecuted.

`RelationshipMemoryInvariantPropertyTest` checks irreversible milestone reward/unlock retention after 64 subsequent hostile hits for each tracked bond, additive permanent history marks despite reversed live streak, and mutual exclusivity of kindness/tender streaks across 512 seeded mixed outcomes. `RelationshipProgressionInvariantTest` verifies encounters alone/hits cannot manufacture trust; clean passes and sustained clean outcomes can progress trust/milestone; hits delay but do not erase later trust; saturated affinity/harm counters preserve severity and ranking. `RenderHotPathReuseTest` checks caller-owned mutable lighting/cinematic/Bloom resolvers preserve object identity, status strings are reused until the displayed tenth changes, and parallax shaders avoid rebuilds for unchanged parameters while rebuilding on material changes. No tests run.

`ReturnMomentsArithmeticIntegrationTest` verifies rough-run streak saturation/increment/reset, protects against signed overflow when a clock rolls backward from `Long.MAX_VALUE`, and pins the long-absence greeting threshold at 36 hours. `ReturnMomentStatePersistenceTest` checks saturated streak values survive overflowed updates and negative persisted timestamps/streaks sanitize to safe defaults on load. Source read only.

`ReturnMomentsSystemTest` is a broad Robolectric composition suite. It checks run-outcome and greeting acknowledgment updates remain serializable under 50 concurrent races; first-of-day, 36-hour absence and repeatable preview/acknowledgment semantics; and priority-selected Garden moments across repeat friend, worn/rough runs, gentle/fearful mood, mercy/kindness/clean-pass depth, Bloom conversions, route tier, milestone bond/high score, repeat killer/strained bonds, and peace. Concrete expected titles include `Welcome Back`, `Still Here`, `You Came Back Worn`, `Still Gentle Here`, `Nothing Closed`, `Take A Breath`, `Kept Company`, `Peace Shared`, `Bond Kept Open`, `Still Tender`, `You Were Already Bracing`, `Held At A Distance`, `Nothing In You Unclenched`, `Same Shadow`, `It Came Home With You`, `Bloom Still Clings`, `Nothing Disturbed`, `Stayed Gentle`, `Peace Held`, `Mercy Was Noticed`, `Mercy Kept The Light`, `Mercy Learned You`, `Mercy Stayed Open`, `Kept Finding You`, `Clear Company`, `Clear All The Way`, `Known Height`, `Peace Kept`, and `Kindness Stayed`. The suite also proves preview is read-only/repeatable and only explicit presentation acknowledgment advances saved clocks. It was fully read but not executed.

`RunFlavorPresentationTest` checks live collision cue escalation from repeat killer/relationship context, milestone cue priority across peaceful route/high score/shared world opinion/bond reactions, entity-aware mercy and pass copy, expanded pass bubble options, and progress cue text/size by tracked value/route. `RunModeTest` pins ordinary mode's persistence, ghost, random-spawn, cue and playback permissions and ensures all deterministic modes suppress them; parsing absent/unknown/NORMAL-as-scenario defaults to debug isolation while capture/profile modes remain explicit. `RunOutcomePersistenceCoordinatorTest` checks once-only terminal commit ordering, nonpersistent token consumption preventing later retroactive writes, per-run reset reopening ownership, summary persistence without replacing non-best ghost, failed ghost publication with no separate distance write, and malformed distance excluded from ghost promotion. Tests were read only.

`RunOutcomeRecoveryIntegrationTest` uses real SharedPreferences recovery and summary snapshot adapters to ensure startup completes a persisted multi-domain journal idempotently when mood already applied, recognizes applied summary/route snapshots, and retains conflicting evidence while blocking new writes. `RunOutcomeRecoveryStoreTest` checks namespaced journal round trip of all state/summary/route fields, raw malformed values remain loadable for canonical sanitization, empty/clear semantics, malformed schema/phase/types/missing fields/negative route counts retained as corrupt, and internally inconsistent replacement rejected without clobbering prior evidence. Not run.

`RunOutcomeRecoveryTransitionIntegrationTest` compares journal transition calculations directly with canonical `ForestMoodSystem`, `ReturnMomentsSystem` and snapshot-store route increments across every mood, rough/calm return outcomes, saturation, and `NONE`/maximum route ceilings. `RunOutcomeSummarySnapshotStoreTest` checks sanitized summary plus route count save, replay idempotency, each persistent route-tier key, preserving compatibility `NONE` counter behavior, and saturation at the canonical recoverable route cap. Both read fully and unexecuted.

`RunResetManagerTest` checks the dying settle fraction, restart reset, state-gated transition-clock behavior, NaN/negative/infinite delta rejection, and exact bounded terminal restart alpha. `RunResetPartitionInvariantTest` compares dying and restart/fade outcomes under 1–257 finite elapsed-time partitions, requiring identical terminal states and at most one alpha unit of intermediate rounding drift. `RunSessionSequencePropertyTest` drives 64 seeded traces × 512 ordinary events through fresh effect coordinators, asserting planner before/after/effect consistency, transactional publication only after accepted plans, rejection purity, and no failed sink; it also checks the canonical run→rest→garden→menu sequence and stale callbacks, plan determinism across all snapshot/event pairs, and every failure position of multi-effect routes with deterministic healthy retry. `RunSessionTransitionCoordinatorTest` pins effect order and adoption, accepted idempotent debug no-op, first-failure/exception short-circuit, and rejected-event sink purity. Fully read; not executed.

`RuntimeAssetContractTest` decodes each required sprite sheet, checks divisibility by authored frame count and dimensions/pixel budget, and verifies packaged font plus mandatory audio resources are nonempty. `RuntimeCadenceClockTest` checks partition-invariant elapsed/accessibility polling at 30/60/120 Hz, one consume per interval, retained overshoot, no backlog burst after long disablement, malformed delta no-op, reset, and positive interval validation. `RuntimeWorkloadTelemetryTest` checks independent current/peak counts for five workload families, concurrent snapshot pair coherence, negative clamping, and reset. `SafeContentTransformTest` pins logical-space identity at zero insets, aspect-preserving safe-area placement, coordinate round-trip, edge clamping, deterministic nonfinite coordinate handling and pathological inset fallback. `SafeProgressionArithmeticTest` checks negative normalization and saturating counters plus rollback/overflow-safe elapsed comparisons. Read fully; not run.

`SanctuaryAtmosphereTest` pins independent modifier accumulation for fireflies, petals, bloom patches, lantern/ground glow, mist and canopy; it exercises severe negative persisted inputs and `Int.MAX_VALUE` inputs against bounded visual outputs. `SaveIntegrityNamespaceAdversarialTest` ensures future-version primary data remains byte-for-byte untouched while corrupt compatibility preferences are repaired in isolation, and malformed schema types migrate as legacy without deleting unknown keys. `SaveManagerDerivedCounterTest` checks wrong-typed/negative per-species counters normalize to zero and extreme counters clamp before overflow and stay saturated after increments. `SaveManagerGhostRecoveryTest` verifies ghost availability and loading from primary or compatibility `.bak` when the base artifact is missing. Fully read; not executed.

`SeedOrbSpawnPolicyTest` checks visible, jump-reachable vertical staging and ahead-of-player X placement for ordinary/full-height encounters; deterministic 5×5 surface geometry sweep; actual Player/Orb jump collection under max speed and left jitter; speed-aware lead; pending-encounter corridor exclusion; and malformed geometry fail-closed finite ordered output. `SessionArcComposerTest` exercises menu/rest/Garden presentation assembled from last-run mood, return-moment previews, persistent memory, route tier, repeat friend and milestone bonds, biome friendship, homecoming consequences, costume, and first-start/fearful tone. `SfxManagerTest` covers bounded transient stream ledger, Bloom event volume/rate relationships and ready-sample fallback selection. `SoundSampleReadinessTest` pins generation-scoped asynchronous completion, failure removal, invalidation of stale callbacks, generation reset and zero-ID rejection. `SpawnFairnessEnvelopeTest` checks declared geometric minimum lead vs full jump flight, broad production grid bounds, opening guide reaction-time progression/expiry, monotonic bounded speed and malformed input fallback. `SpawnPacingTest` checks distance-only post-tutorial gaps, opening reaction-time conversion, max-speed full-jump action lead including frame admission/gesture/recovery margin, and malformed/extreme speed/time handling. `SpriteSheetTest` covers frame timer remainder, malformed timing, huge bounded deltas, terminal nonlooping state, no-op invalid draw bounds, bitmap-shared independent playback copies, and frame geometry validation. `SpriteSizingTest` tests aspect preservation, caller minimums, invalid/nonfinite dimensions/minimums and overflow fallback. All read; not run.

`StoryFragmentCatalogueInvariantTest` enumerates every `EntityType` to ensure deterministic nonblank bounded creature thought, safe persistent page IDs, and safe rest/Garden/weather text over rich, empty and strained histories; page count must match unique unlocked IDs. `StoryFragmentSystemTest` covers read-only Garden previews versus actual page-unlocking presentation; rest/Garden/weather fragment families and memory IDs for repeat killer/friend, creature, biome, mood, route, Bloom, clean rhythm, kindness/warmth, milestone, caution/strained bond and same-shadow histories; thoughts for tracked and untracked entity types; and contextual route/biome names. `SurfaceResizePolicyTest` checks only initialized dimension-bound systems request activity recreation after actual size changes, avoiding startup/invalid-size and same-size loops. `SwayComponentTest` checks invalid frame inputs, wind fallback/no rewind, extreme finite bounded motion, phase reset, and constructor validation. `SweptCoreOverlapTest` proves continuous synchronized moving player/core contact catches horizontal tunnel and Lily/Hedgehog physical contacts without diagonal-union false positives, treats touching boundary as non-overlap, rejects malformed geometry, and preserves terminal collision precedence without mercy/pass leakage. `TerminalHitFeedbackPresenterIntegrationTest` compares queued bubble/flavor text against canonical `RunFlavorPresentation` exactly once and rejects nonfinite anchors. `TreeEncounterFlavorTest` pins familiarity and repeated-hit copy for willow, jacaranda, bamboo and cherry. `WorldOpinionPresentationTest` maps repeated kindness to Trusting and repeat-killer/fearful history to Shadowed with corresponding run bubble. Fully read; not executed.

## Android instrumentation implementation review

`InstrumentationStateReset` resets primary and compatibility save namespaces, feedback settings, run-outcome recovery journals, in-memory ghost persistence coordination, and `ghost_run*` files. `AccessibilityVirtualNodeInstrumentedTest` launches the real Activity/GameView, waits for layout and rendered frames, then checks provider node exposure/actionability changes across menu/settings, the reduced-motion toggle, and close navigation. `ForestJournalLifecycleInstrumentedTest` seeds wrong-typed values, launches the actual activity to exercise startup integrity repair, then verifies section selection survives recreation without writing progression beyond schema repair.

`HardwareCoreFlowCaptureTest` is a physical-device visual evidence harness: clears old MediaStore and shell capture directory output; configures deterministic screenshot-capture mode through the production debug-launch boundary; captures scheduled PixelCopy images for opening, Bloom and ghost scenarios; and drives the rest-loop through live, dying, game-over and Garden-return screenshots. Its helpers wait for state/frame/surface readiness, inject taps, perform PixelCopy with bounded latch, publish PNGs through pending MediaStore entries, and inspect the live scenario owners. `HardwareInputLatencyProfileTest` uses instrumented pointer events (tap and downward swipe) after render warmup, samples app receipt-to-first-posted-response-frame telemetry, checks sample/percentile ordering, and writes device/build/refresh metadata JSON. Its doc explicitly bounds measurement: it is not touchscreen sensor-to-display scanout latency.

`HardwarePerformanceProfileTest` is a physical-device profiling harness, not a universal threshold gate. For opening, Bloom, each entity family, ghost readability and maximum-frame ghost persistence scenarios, it clears state, begins a bounded telemetry session, launches performance-profile mode, warms the render loop, stops the thread before in-place monitor reset, re-applies scenario, then polls while replaying scenarios until duration expires. It checks frame/sample/percentile/heap coherence and replay count; the max ghost case separately awaits and verifies exactly one successful full-capacity write. It emits JSON with device, workload and ghost I/O evidence and status extras. `LifecycleSettingsInstrumentedTest` checks a paused render thread terminates and a different thread resumes frames; single-task scenario intents replace active deterministic scenario without recreating/finishing activity; settings take effect, persist and survive recreation; and malformed save/feedback preferences repair before first frame.

`MainActivityInstrumentedTest` drives the real menu ready-phase flow and gameplay in connected instrumentation. It covers app launch; input-to-play, frame/entity progression and jump; Garden navigation, authoritative plant purchase/currency/progress persistence and return; Bloom activation/invulnerability and conversion; collision→dying→game-over→restart; biome transitions over long-run distances; one update for every declared entity type in valid common staging; and best-run distance/ghost publication followed by reload on a new Activity launch. Test helpers stop/resume the production runtime thread around reflective owner mutations to avoid cross-thread fixture races, transform logical hit targets to device pixels and await explicit state/time-bounded conditions. All 27 instrumentation cases passed on the temporary emulator; physical-device acceptance remains a separate evidence gate.

## Build configuration, Android manifest and resources

`settings.gradle.kts` restricts dependency resolution to Google and Maven Central with `FAIL_ON_PROJECT_REPOS`, declares plugin repositories including Gradle Plugin Portal, names the root `Forest_Run`, and includes only `:app`. Root `build.gradle.kts` pins Android Gradle Plugin 8.13.2 and Kotlin Android 1.9.22. `gradle-wrapper.properties` selects Gradle 8.13 binary distribution. `gradle.properties` sets a 2 GiB daemon heap, UTF-8, AndroidX, official Kotlin style and non-transitive R classes; parallel mode remains commented. `app/build.gradle.kts` resolves release-signing inputs from Gradle properties before environment variables, creates signing only when all four values exist, configures namespace/application ID `com.anurag9000.forestrun`, compile/target SDK 36, min SDK 24, version 1/1.0.0, Java/Kotlin 17, debug `.debug` suffix and non-minified debug, plus minified/resource-shrunk release with optimized default ProGuard and project rules. Compose is disabled; unit tests include Android resources. Runtime dependencies are AppCompat 1.7.0 and Core KTX 1.13.1; JUnit 4.13.2, AndroidX Test Core 1.6.1, Robolectric 4.16, AndroidX JUnit 1.2.1 and Espresso 3.6.1 support tests. Latest execution on Java 21 passed `assembleDebug`, `assembleRelease`, `lintDebug`, `lintRelease`, and `testDebugUnitTest`; Android test APK compilation and the targeted API 36 instrumentation test also passed. A cold emulator run first failed because the public SD-card volume was unmounted and Android later crashed under memory pressure; after mounting that volume and using a 3 GiB emulator, the full 28-test connected suite passed. The targeted release-asset validation instrumentation test also passed. The 85 remaining lint warnings are enumerated in the Gradle reports and are under continued triage, not all confirmed defects.

`app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml` requests only VIBRATE; disables backup/RTL; declares the game category and a temporary Android 16 restricted-resizability compatibility property; sets label/icon/theme; declares internal landscape `ForestJournalActivity`; and exported landscape immersive, keep-screen-on, singleTask launcher `MainActivity` with orientation/screen/keyboard/layout config-change handling and hidden/no-adjust soft input. `res/xml/data_extraction_rules.xml` excludes root, files, databases, shared preferences, external and all four device-protected data domains from both cloud backup and device transfer; `backup_rules.xml` excludes the same domains for legacy backup. Robolectric parses and checks the complete exclusion sets, and API 36 instrumentation verifies the runtime property and game/landscape package metadata. `values/colors.xml`, `values/strings.xml` and `values/themes.xml` define the forest palette, Forest Run label and no-action-bar fullscreen window with forest-deep launch/system-bar background. `drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml` and `ic_launcher_foreground.xml` define concentric forest circles/canopy and willow leaf/vein/seed. `mipmap-anydpi/ic_launcher.xml`, `ic_launcher_round.xml`, `mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml` and `ic_launcher_round.xml` are the four adaptive-icon wrappers; the API-26 variants also set monochrome to foreground, and the round background masks the canopy silhouette. `app/proguard-rules.pro`, wrapper scripts/JAR, and local.properties are separate configuration artifacts; local.properties and generated `.gradle` state are machine-local and excluded from durable semantic mapping.

The 45 packaged visual/audio files comprise 30 asset files (29 sprites: runner girl movement/death/hit strips; five animals; five bird base sprites plus five flying variants; five plants; four trees; and one Press Start 2P font) and 15 raw Ogg files (six music tracks for Bloom/Garden/rest/three run variants and nine effects for bark, Bloom, hit, howl, jump, land, mercy miss, screech and seed ping). `RuntimeAssetValidator`/`audit_runtime_asset_references.py` establish the runtime path contract; release source-asset validation parses image/audio formats and frame geometry. `asset-provenance.json` has five schema-v1 glob rules partitioning fonts, sprites/VFX, raw media, drawables and launcher icons; all are `review-required` with blank provenance/license/attribution/reviewer/date fields, so no asset is asserted approved by this file. Resource file bytes are not reproduced in this textual map.

Exact binary inventory (paths relative to `app/src/main`): `assets/sprites/char/runner_girl_technical_48frame.png`, `runner_girl_jump_48frame.png`, `runner_girl_duck_48frame.png`, `runner_girl_hit_sequence.png`, `runner_girl_death_sequence.png`; `assets/sprites/animals/cat_4frames.png`, `dog_4frames.png`, `fox_4frames.png`, `hedgehog_4frames.png`, `wolf_4frames.png`; `assets/sprites/birds/chickadee_4frames.png`, `chickadee_flying.png`, `duck_4frames.png`, `duck_flying.png`, `eagle_4frames.png`, `eagle_flying.png`, `owl_4frames.png`, `owl_flying.png`, `tit_4frames.png`, `tit_flying.png`; `assets/sprites/plants/cactus_4frames.png`, `eucalyptus_4frames.png`, `hyacinth_4frames.png`, `lily_of_valley_4frames.png`, `vanilla_orchid_4frames.png`; `assets/sprites/trees/bamboo_4frames.png`, `cherry_blossom_4frames.png`, `jacaranda_4frames.png`, `weeping_willow_4frames.png`; font `assets/fonts/PressStart2P-Regular.ttf`. Raw media: `res/raw/music_bloom.ogg`, `music_garden.ogg`, `music_rest.ogg`, `music_run_1.ogg`, `music_run_2.ogg`, `music_run_3.ogg`, `sfx_bark.ogg`, `sfx_bloom.ogg`, `sfx_hit.ogg`, `sfx_howl.ogg`, `sfx_jump.ogg`, `sfx_land.ogg`, `sfx_mercy_miss.ogg`, `sfx_screech.ogg`, `sfx_seed_ping.ogg`.

`app/proguard-rules.pro` keeps enum `values()`/`valueOf(String)` members for persisted enum-name compatibility and strips Android verbose/debug/info logging side effects in optimized release builds. The checked-in Unix/Windows Gradle launchers resolve their own root, validate Java availability, set a small client JVM default, convert Windows-like path arguments where needed, and delegate to `GradleWrapperMain` using the checked-in wrapper JAR. Root `.gitignore` excludes local IDE/Gradle/build state, local SDK configuration, Python caches/virtual environments, generated release/store/screenshot outputs, APK/AAB files and signing material; `app/.gitignore` separately excludes `/build`. `local.properties` only points to the local Android SDK and is ignored; generated `.gradle` cache property files are not authored source.

## Repository scripts and build controls

### Read so far

`scripts/strict_json.py` is the shared fail-closed evidence parser: rejects empty/oversized/BOM/invalid UTF-8 payloads, duplicate object keys, nonstandard constants, oversized integer literals and excessive nesting before parsing; validates the parsed tree for finite floats and allowed JSON types. File reads resolve and stat the explicit path, require a regular bounded file, open with no-follow/nonblocking flags where available, compare device/inode/mode/size/timestamps around the exact descriptor read and path afterward, then parse the bounded bytes. `StrictJsonError` carries the stable failure boundary; invalid limits are programmer `ValueError`s.

`scripts/acceptance_test_support.py` constructs a deterministic schema-v2 acceptance trace fixture from the authored Kotlin scenario/input contract. It rejects negative injected dispatch lateness, requires nonempty authored input, derives exact sequential event timestamps/actions plus scenario and trace hashes, updates the chosen evidence-file path/hash in the bundle, materializes standard bundle fixtures through the validator helper, and writes the trace bytes. `scripts/audit_no_trainable_surface.py` is a CLI wrapper around `training_control.forest_no_trainable_authority.audit`: adds repository root to import path, atomically publishes a JSON report through a unique same-directory temporary file, rejects symlinked/non-directory report path components, prints canonical compact JSON, and returns 2 unless the audit is complete.

`scripts/audit_runtime_asset_references.py` parses the Kotlin asset declaration, recursively inventories packaged asset files, rejects symlinks/nonregular/unsafe paths, requires exact declared-versus-packaged path equality, hashes all files, and rejects byte-identical duplicate groups except the five explicit base/flying bird aliases. It supports repository-root and JSON CLI output; its evidence reports declared/package counts and duplicate groups. `scripts/scenario_source_contract.py` reconstructs deterministic scenarios from Kotlin without importing app code: bounded stable UTF-8 reads; balanced-parenthesis extraction respecting escaped strings and nested line/block comments; strict regex parsing for typed encounter/input steps; Kotlin Float32 conversion, Kotlin-compatible round-to-long microsecond/micropixel normalization; length-prefixed canonical byte formats and SHA-256 scenario/trace-contract hashes. `load_trace_contract` binds both scenario and debug-input source files. These five scripts were read fully; no scripts/tests were executed.

`build_creative_asset_inventory.py` validates source artwork and runtime ownership first, then inventories every file under app asset/raw roots with category, byte length and SHA-256. Canonical compact sorted JSON binds asset records to candidate commit and inventory digest while marking every provenance/license review unresolved; writer uses a sibling temp file plus replacement. `build_declared_dependency_inventory.py` parses exact root/app Gradle plugin/module declarations, wrapper distribution version and pinned CI Python requirements from four hash-recorded regular sources; it insists on exactly two plugins, at least one Maven module, wrapper version, and every Python requirement exact-pinned. Output is explicitly direct-only (not transitive/SBOM/license/vulnerability evidence), candidate-bound and canonically hashed, and published via fsync'ed temporary file plus atomic replace with symlink checks.

`build_resolved_dependency_sbom.py` consumes named Gradle resolved reports, parses final Maven coordinates including `-> selectedVersion`, rejects conflicting versions per module, hashes each report, deduplicates coordinates, builds stable Maven purls and CycloneDX 1.6 components, then derives deterministic component-set hash and UUIDv5 serial from candidate/configuration identity. It records scope/limitations and atomically publishes canonical JSON. `build_release_evidence_index.py` is the principal indexer: validates lowercase candidate SHA, canonical UTC timestamp, kind and relative path/suffix; recursively extracts only explicit candidate identities (including commit keys only inside candidate/build objects); bounds entries/file/JSON sizes and uses `strict_json`; rejects duplicate kinds/paths, symlinks/nonregular/out-of-root paths, hardlink aliases, output/input aliasing, candidate mismatch and missing required bound kinds; hashes stable opened inodes into sorted entries and evidence-set digest. Publication checks output containment and hardlink identity before/after mkdir/temp write, fsyncs file and directory, then atomically replaces. `build_stable_release_evidence_index.py` freezes each evidence file by descriptor-based safe read and records identity/bytes/hash, rejects repeated hardlinked inputs, builds in a temporary tree, rereads and compares every original snapshot, publishes, then invokes the independent verifier; failed post-publication verification removes the output and fails. These five builders were read fully; none were run.

`compile_device_acceptance.py` reads a bounded strict-JSON draft, derives only schema/timestamp and candidate/evidence file hashes, preserves captured store-delivery/session build identities, validates the finished bundle using the acceptance validator and transactionally publishes manifest plus optional summary with rollback backups. `compile_human_acceptance.py` follows the same compiler pattern but hashes the referenced device manifest and every human review evidence file while preserving reviewer/check facts. `compile_installed_identity_matrix.py` validates and hashes the linked device acceptance first, derives candidate/signing/artifact fields from that verified manifest, hashes per-session installed identity records, then validates and transactionally publishes. `compile_play_delivery_evidence.py` validates the installed identity matrix, derives candidate identity from it, requires exactly the validator's five evidence kinds, hashes each, and validates before staging/rollback publication; entered release/track/review assertions remain human sourced. `compile_release_governance.py` hashes linked device and human acceptance manifests plus exactly required evidence kinds, adds schema/timestamp, validates all assertions, and transactionally publishes. These compilers share the critical boundary: evidence digests and schema metadata are derived, while human/device facts remain supplied and validators reject mismatch.

`aggregate_device_acceptance.py` validates each candidate and optional baseline bundle, re-reads manifests to detect mutation during trace validation, and independently validates deterministic scenario traces against current Kotlin source contracts. It computes normalized physical-device and full-profile identities, per-required-device-class and global distributions for latency/frame/memory/crash metrics, worst-case threshold headroom, durations, trace contract set and a comparison-matrix digest. Baseline comparison requires identical class/session/profile matrices and trace contracts before emitting mean/max deltas; it states no invented tolerance. Output is prevented from aliasing manifests, artifacts or evidence by path or inode and is atomically fsync-published. These six scripts were read fully; no CLI or test was run.

`validate_device_acceptance.py` is the acceptance authority for schema v2. It enforces exact canonical repository/branch/application identity, signed candidate artifact/store delivery agreement and certificate digests; mandatory five device classes and seven scenario families; unique normalized physical-device/class assignment; UTC session timings/duration consistency; exact per-session candidate build/version/signing/install identity; all required scenario pass records with nonempty unique hashed evidence; numeric/performance thresholds and minimum duration; exact manual-check and approval key sets/states; distinct reviewers; and chronology. When given an evidence root it rejects traversal/symlink/nonregular/oversized files, hashes candidate artifact and evidence, and detects path/hardlink reuse. `load_and_validate` guards bounded regular manifest reads and strict JSON before invoking the model validator. CLI returns compact valid/invalid summaries and optionally atomically writes summary JSON. This validator was read completely; not executed.

`validate_human_acceptance.py` binds schema-v2 human review to a separately validated device acceptance candidate and session set. It requires exact gameplay, accessibility, and presentation check maps per review, allows N/A only for an explicit small allowlist and requires cutout/aspect checks on the cutout device; all mandatory device classes must be covered. It validates candidate/artifact/certificate/version parity, session class, reviewer/time fields and completion chronology, required immutable evidence path/hash records, no path/hardlink reuse, and at least two distinct final reviewers drawn from review authors. Summary exposes candidate, device acceptance digest, review/class/reviewer and evidence counts; it never invents a pass.

`validate_installed_candidate_identity.py` is the collector's independent schema authority. Its strict typed helpers require exact object keys, reject bool-as-int, trim/bound strings and reject control characters, require canonical UTC-Z timestamps and safe POSIX relative evidence paths. Validation binds schema-v1 candidate repository/main/SHA/application/version/app-signing cert/Play installer; requires device serial hash and physical properties; checks installed app identity/version/installer/certificate; validates a unique APK set with exactly one `base.apk`, lowercase digests, positive sizes and same signer; and fixes claims so Play installer attribution is true but specific-track verification is false. Evidence resolution rejects lexical/resolved root escape and symlink traversal; bounded regular files are hashed with before/after metadata stability and duplicate inode rejection. Every APK record must match the corresponding pulled `apks/<name>` hash and byte size. `load_and_validate` lstat/size-checks the manifest around strict JSON parsing and returns a compact summary binding the manifest's own SHA. CLI may atomically publish that summary. `validate_installed_identity_matrix.py` independently binds one installed identity record to every session in validated device acceptance. Candidate/repository/version/artifact and both certificates are copied by equality from that authority; every linked identity manifest and evidence digest is revalidated, and each record's device manufacturer/model/fingerprint/SDK must equal its physical session. It rejects repeated session/path/inode/device serial identities, enforces capture near session time (within 24 hours of start or completion) and no capture after matrix generation, and bounds/stable-reads strict JSON manifests. `validate_play_delivery_evidence.py` revalidates that matrix and requires an exact candidate match, Google Play internal track, valid release id, all five upload/release/eligibility/install/update assertions, monotonic UTC event times, five unique hash-bound external evidence files without symlink/hardlink aliases, and approved distinct operator/reviewer after update. It records external assertions only and never contacts Play Console.

`validate_release_governance.py` cross-validates device and human acceptance, installed identity matrix, Play delivery, and all exact required governance evidence kinds against a shared candidate/artifact/certificate/version identity. It validates HTTPS privacy-policy URL, enabled private vulnerability reporting, approved decisions across 20 named domains, hash-safe evidence paths, release notes containing exact candidate/artifact/certificate/version identifiers, and a distinct accountable final owner/reviewer after every domain decision. Its docstring explicitly leaves legal sufficiency, policy correctness, licensing compatibility, artwork quality, and security posture as human judgments. These four validators were read fully; none were run.

`validate_device_acceptance_aggregate.py` independently checks aggregate schema v2 with exact-key/type/hash validation. It requires five mandatory classes, sorted unique physical/profile/session IDs, mathematically ordered distributions and class/session counts, sorted trace contract hashes, candidate canonical app identity, and global minima/maxima/weighted means consistent with per-class summaries. It verifies matrix digest, nonnegative headroom, optional baseline delta maps and exact frozen interpretation; file entry uses bounded strict JSON. This makes the aggregator's output self-validating rather than trusting the producer. Both this validator and the 968-line device-acceptance validator were read in full; neither was executed.

`validate_manifest_scenario_traces.py` revalidates the acceptance manifest and scans every evidence path whose basename is `scenario-trace-*.json`; each matching trace is checked against candidate commit/artifact plus current Kotlin source-derived scenario/input definitions. It can require at least one trace and returns sorted per-trace contract/event/lateness/payload digest evidence. `validate_scenario_trace.py` bounds and stable-reads trace JSON, enforces exact schema/event keys and limits, verifies candidate hashes and authored scenario/trace digests, then checks event count exactly matches the nonempty input contract, contiguous sequence, chronological schedule/dispatch, no dispatch-before-schedule, exact lateness arithmetic, allowed action names and exact per-index scheduled time/action. This binds capture evidence to current deterministic Kotlin source. `validate_supply_chain_evidence.py` cross-binds direct dependency inventory and CycloneDX 1.6 SBOM to one commit, recomputes entry/component digests, checks declared-source and resolved-report provenance hash shape, canonical Maven purls, exactly release and Android-test runtime configurations, and presence of each declared implementation/androidTestImplementation module in resolved components. These three validators were read fully; not run.

`verify_main_candidate.py` uses subprocess Git probes to require repository-root execution, a completely clean worktree including untracked files, named `main` branch, exact `HEAD == refs/heads/main`, valid full SHA, and optional equality to a frozen expected SHA. It outputs either compact JSON identity or human status and fails closed via `CandidateVerificationError`. `verify_origin_main.sh` normalizes HTTPS/SSH/scp-style GitHub origin URLs, requires canonical origin, fetches only origin/main, and requires local HEAD/local main/origin main all point to identical commits. It is a live network/repository-state release gate and was read, not run.

`verify_release_evidence_index.py` independently verifies bounded strict-JSON index schema, canonical second-precision UTC and candidate, sorted unique kinds/paths/bindings, candidate-binding semantics, counts and canonical set hash; required kinds must be present and bound. It safely stable-reads the index and each indexed file through no-follow descriptors, checks root containment, inode stability, evidence byte/hash and reconstructed candidate bindings, rejects index/evidence aliasing and hardlink-reused evidence, then rereads the index to detect mutation during verification. Its summary includes total bytes and both set/index hashes. Read fully; not executed.

`release_artifact_verifier.py` provides injectable subprocess runner/tool resolution and typed bundle/signature results. ZIP structure validation bounds entry count and expanded sizes, rejects duplicate/unsafe/control-character/drive/colon/traversal names, symlink and encrypted entries, runs CRC validation, requires nonempty BundleConfig/manifest and at least one base DEX. Bundle identity is queried with apkanalyzer and compared exactly with expected application/version/name. Signature verification requires jarsigner's complete verified marker with no unsigned-entry markers, obtains bundle and configured keystore certificate SHA-256 via keytool, and requires equality; an explicitly allowed unsigned mode only returns unsigned evidence when signing inputs are absent. Read fully; tools were not invoked.

`verify_release_summary.py` validates machine and human release summaries as a candidate-bound projection: main SHA/application/version identity and typed dry-run overrides; exact required graphics/metadata paths with current hashes, byte/character counts; complete required audio names; at least four unique final screenshot PNGs with unique hashes and candidate/debug-package binding; and either explicit skip-build absence of bundle/R8 claims or canonical bundle/mapping paths with file hashes, exact package/version identity, signature state and coherent positive R8 class counts. It also requires the human summary to state candidate/application and disclose skip-build or exact bundle hash/signature status. Path resolution rejects symlink traversal and root escape. Read fully; not run.

`verify_release_source_assets.py` parses runtime asset constants from Kotlin and required audio names as a literal Python AST value, rejects unsafe/duplicate paths and validates all bounded asset bytes independently of Android packaging. PNG verification checks signature/chunk order/CRC/IHDR color-depth geometry/palette/critical chunks/IEND, bounded zlib expansion and scanline bytes, plus filename-declared sprite-frame divisibility. Font validation checks sfnt signature, bounded unique in-file table records, required cmap/head/hhea/hmtx/maxp/name tables and core table invariants. Ogg verifies page lacing, CRC, per-logical-stream BOS/sequence/EOS, and Vorbis/Opus first packet; WAV validates RIFF sizing and fmt/data chunks/format bounds; MP3 scans a bounded prefix after optional ID3; M4A validates box bounds and ftyp/moov/mdat. It validates every raw audio file (not only required names), then ensures every required stem exists exactly once. Read fully; not run.

`validate_release_readiness.py` orchestrates the final candidate gate across physical acceptance, human acceptance, installed identity matrix, Play delivery, governance, store metadata and release evidence index. It validates each layer independently, requires a common expected commit, artifact and upload/app-signing certificates, checks every inter-manifest reference by digest, then verifies the release index requires all listed JSON-bound evidence kinds and includes the signed binary bundle as an explicitly unbound digest equal to the accepted artifact. It rejects root escape/symlink traversal, hashes referenced manifests after validation to catch identity drift, and optionally atomically emits a readiness summary. It creates no evidence; it cross-checks the independent authorities. Read fully; not run.

`prepare_main_release.sh` is the top-level release transaction: optionally validates/exports JAVA_HOME runtime, verifies canonical origin/main and a clean local main candidate, checks both SHAs agree, validates source assets, approved provenance, store graphics, listing parity and metadata, backs up any existing machine/human summaries, runs `prepare_play_release.py`, verifies resulting summaries and unchanged clean candidate, then rechecks origin/main. Its EXIT trap restores prior summaries if any later gate fails. `aggregate_device_acceptance_bundle.sh` checks argument/tool availability and output/source collisions, preflights candidate/baseline strict JSON, requires deterministic traces in each manifest, stages aggregation in output directory, verifies staged strict JSON plus independent aggregate schema, then delegates source-bound publication to `publish_device_acceptance_aggregate.py`; cleanup always removes leftover staged output. Both shell wrappers were read; not executed.

`prepare_play_release.py` is the candidate-specific release builder. It enforces clean Git tree and frozen SHA, parses application/version/SDK identity from Gradle, handles explicit placeholder/unsigned/skip-build dry-run overrides, reads secrets only from environment or external Gradle properties, validates exact graphics manifest hashes/dimensions, metadata length/placeholders, curated screenshot sidecars/candidate/device/package/order/dedup/dimensions and required audio resources. The normal path invokes Java 21+ Gradle clean/unit tests/release lint/AAB tasks, rechecks the source tree, validates bundle ZIP/identity/signature using `release_artifact_verifier`, and requires nonzero R8 app classes plus renamed classes. It records machine JSON and a human summary enumerating automated evidence and still-required manual checks; the outer shell wrapper provides rollback on failure. Read fully; no release build or related command was executed.

`publish_device_acceptance_aggregate.py` is the final source-bound publication gate. It stable-reads and validates candidate/baseline acceptance manifests, trace contracts, every referenced artifact/evidence digest, and staged aggregate schema; rejects staged/output aliasing protected sources; validates staged summary identity against final candidate/baseline; reconstructs the entire aggregate from source manifests and requires exact object equality; rereads staged bytes and every protected-source stat identity immediately before `os.replace`, then fsyncs the output directory. The stage must share the output directory for atomic publication. Read fully; not run.

`verify_store_listing_parity.py` extracts exactly one canonical fenced text block under each named `docs/STORE_LISTING.md` heading after bounded UTF-8, LF and NFC checks, validates metadata structurally, then requires byte-for-text equality for title/short/full descriptions. `verify_store_graphics.py` admits a real nonsymlink directory, strict-loads exact graphics manifest schema/candidate/generator, hashes a fixed source set (generator, font and representative sprites), requires exact source/output names and no extra directory entries, verifies bounded RGB/RGBA PNGs at exact feature/promo dimensions, and matches every mode/size/digest. `verify_store_metadata.py` validates exact locale text files for UTF-8/NFC/LF, no BOM/control/template/placeholder content, line policy and length constraints; finalization removes an old manifest and atomically writes candidate-bound facts, while verification strictly checks exact manifest schema and current character/byte/line/hash facts. All three were read; not run.

`verify_curated_screenshot_set.py` validates ordered unique curation manifest entries and capture-session identity, parses every final PNG itself with size/pixel/decompression bounds, CRC/chunk/IHDR/scanline checks and landscape minimums, then validates each capture sidecar against raw-file/scenario/image dimensions+hash, candidate, APK, device, package/activity and readiness mode; requires exact PNG/sidecar set, consistent capture identity, nonduplicated images and session time after captures. `verify_raw_screenshot_set.py` applies the corresponding rules to an exact raw directory set (all declared raw PNGs, sidecars and session only), validates unique image bytes and binds session candidate plus origin/main, APK/device/package/activity, screenshot count and timestamp. `screenshot_capture_evidence.py` defines the typed capture identity and shared strict sidecar decoder; `write_screenshot_capture_evidence.py` enforces supported scenario/run/package/activity/readiness marker, valid hashes, finite settle period and UTC time, validates PNG using the curated parser, and atomically writes sidecar JSON. These four scripts were read; not run.

`curate_store_screenshots.py` validates the curated source manifest, inspects raw images through Pillow for valid bounded landscape size, luma variance and optional reviewed dark-bar exception, computes SHA-256 and dHash, rejects duplicate/near-duplicate images and requires consistent dimensions/capture identity, checks sidecar hashes/scenarios, stages copied PNG+sidecars, and swaps final directory with backup/rollback. The generated report preserves capture and review context and states marketing/meaning still requires human review. `finalize_screenshot_capture_session.py` checks exact PNG/sidecar correspondence/count, unique scenario, strict PNG and sidecar contents, common requested candidate/origin/APK/device/package/activity identity, and session timestamp after all captures, then atomically writes `capture-session.json`. Read fully; not run.

`capture_store_screenshots.sh` verifies required tools, canonical origin/main and clean candidate equality; validates source assets, builds the exact-main debug APK, hashes it, resolves exactly one authorized ADB device (or requires explicit serial), clears prior raw capture files, installs/clears the app, and captures eight authored deterministic scenarios. Each capture force-stops/starts the target in screenshot mode, requires process and explicit scenario-ready log marker, waits the authored settle period, verifies foreground activity before/after screencap, and calls the sidecar writer. It finalizes exact session evidence then rechecks both clean local candidate and origin/main for drift. The script deletes prior raw screenshot/session evidence in the selected output directory before capture. Read fully; not executed.

`compare_visual_regression.py` is a diagnostic image comparator, explicitly not a replacement for device, accessibility, or human acceptance. It bounds PNG bytes/pixels, rejects symlink roots/files/parents and path escape, hashes before and after decode to detect mutation, requires equal dimensions and computes mean absolute RGB channel delta, p95 per-pixel maximum channel delta, and proportion of pixels exceeding tolerance. Manifest mode chooses only `raw_file` or `final_file`, compares every manifest scenario, and aggregates maxima/failures. CLI thresholds have finite/range checks; result JSON is sorted and atomically replaced. Exit codes distinguish valid (0), regression (1), and invalid input (2). Read fully; not run.

`collect_installed_candidate_identity.py` requires a clean verified local main candidate matching freshly fetched canonical origin/main before and after collection. Its helpers normalize HTTPS/SSH/scp origin forms, resolve adb/apkanalyzer/apksigner from PATH or SDK roots (selecting numerically greatest build-tools), and strictly parse authorized device rows, unique base/split APK paths, package summary, installer and certificate digest. Collection validates canonical candidate SHA, version, package and expected Play certificate/installer, requires output absent/empty, writes raw command outputs, selects exactly one authorized device unless explicitly chosen, pulls every APK split, verifies each with apksigner, extracts base identity with apkanalyzer and hashes bytes/sizes. It records device manufacturer/model/name/SDK/fingerprint, hashes the serial instead of publishing it, binds each evidence file by relative path and SHA, and asserts Play installer attribution while setting `specific_play_track_verified=false`. The manifest is fsync-written to a sibling temporary and replaced; origin/main is rechecked and an independent `validate_installed_candidate_identity.load_and_validate` must accept it or the manifest is removed. It writes no artifact proving a specific Play track.

`collect_input_latency_profile.sh` binds physical input-to-posted-frame instrumentation to a clean local candidate identical to origin/main and one explicitly selected/sole authorized device. It clears the app's remote report directory, records device/display/input diagnostics, invokes the dedicated instrumentation class, pulls JSON reports, and optionally evaluates them against a hashed threshold manifest; without reviewed limits it writes a PENDING acceptance note. It rechecks candidate and remote main after capture. `evaluate_input_latency_profiles.py` strictly loads bounded threshold/report JSON, checks schema, device/scenario/refresh/action counts, percentile ordering and component-versus-total latency, selects the unique most-specific device/refresh profile, then checks minimum samples, dropped-action ratio and four p95 stage/total limits. It reports PASS/FAIL per input and returns distinct invalid configuration status.

`collect_performance_profiles.sh` follows the same candidate/device binding, records device properties and before/after health snapshots, runs a selected physical profiling instrumentation test, optionally captures Perfetto, pulls structured reports and diagnostics, optionally evaluates candidate-specific limits, and rechecks candidate/origin after capture. Perfetto is opt-in because tracing perturbs timing; missing limits remain PENDING. `evaluate_performance_profiles.py` strictly parses bounded thresholds/reports, validates internal frame/slow-ratio/percentile/heap/workload/ghost-write telemetry consistency, chooses the unique most-specific device/scenario/refresh profile, and checks frame latency, slow-frame ratio, heap, and optional ghost write limits. It expands files/directories deterministically, prints per-report violations and totals, and distinguishes failed thresholds from invalid evidence/configuration. These five collectors/evaluators were read fully; not run.

`compile_device_acceptance_bundle.sh` wraps draft compilation with strict JSON preflight/postflight, device-acceptance schema validation, and at-least-one manifest scenario-trace validation; optional generation time is forwarded to the compiler. `run_connected_validation.sh` parses only the candidate SHA and explicit origin-check bypass, defaults to emulator serial/port, bounds readiness timeout, requires adb/timeout/git, checks exact requested `HEAD` plus clean worktree and optionally canonical origin/main before work. It polls device state, boot completion, framework package path, package listing and Settings provider; on failures its EXIT trap captures bounded diagnostics. It runs connected instrumentation while excluding only the physical-performance `@LargeTest`, then rechecks HEAD, clean worktree and optional origin/main. `import_final_assets.py` defines immutable per-source mapping records, derives a quantized edge-color palette, flood-fills only connected palette-like edge background, or for character sheets removes light neutral pixels independent of connectivity. It can clear authored support bands, labels alpha-connected components, retains the largest plus nearby components (transitively expanding a subject bound) or components above an authored area fraction, crops with padding, slices explicit grid indices and centers/bottom-aligns processed frames into RGBA strips. Its 30-entry mapping table owns source/destination aliases, frame grids, character cleanup strategy, support-band ratios and component floors. `generate_store_assets.py` builds two deterministic fixed-size Play graphics from selected runtime sprite frames and the pixel font, using nearest-neighbor scaling, gradients, text, shadows and glows. It validates source existence and output PNG dimensions/modes, hashes source/output files, binds a manifest to candidate SHA, and publishes the staged output directory with backup/rollback. `requirements.txt` and `requirements-ci.txt` each pin Pillow 11.3.0. These six files were read fully; none were executed.

`visual_baseline_provenance.py` builds or verifies a deterministic descriptor binding a baseline candidate, curation-manifest hash, selected screenshot filename field, dimensions and screenshot hashes/set digest. It enforces regular non-symlink files/root, bounded PNGs and mutation checks, rebuilds provenance during verification, and explicitly states that provenance is not human visual approval. Publication is canonical JSON via fsynced sibling temporary and replacement. `verify_strict_json_evidence.py` expands positional files/directories (including recursive `*.json` children), resolves and deduplicates/sorts paths, caps the set at 2,048 and rejects empty sets/non-JSON explicit files/missing paths. Each file is loaded by `strict_json.load_file(require_object=True)`; parser exceptions are translated to preflight errors and CLI returns 2, while successful output reports count. It is syntax/ambiguity preflight before domain validation, with semantic schemas left to owning validators. `validate_asset_provenance.py` scans runtime assets/raw/drawable/mipmap trees, rejects symlinks and unsupported entries, validates exact provenance-rule schema/status and approved metadata, requires each asset to match exactly one rule and every rule to be used, and optionally fails on unresolved review-required entries. It reports coverage counts without asserting legal approval beyond registry status. `verify_android_page_size_package.py` snapshots and hashes a bounded APK/AAB ZIP, rejects unsafe/duplicate/encrypted/symlink entries and size bombs, inventories `.so` native libraries, and reports package-level compatibility only when no native code is present; strict legacy mode rejects native code. It explicitly does not replace runtime testing on a 16 KiB page-size device or ELF alignment verification. These four files were read fully; not run.

`test_aggregate_manifest_snapshot.py` constructs a valid traced acceptance bundle, mutates its manifest after trace validation via a patched validator, and expects aggregation's snapshot check to reject it; it also checks missing/nonregular/oversized manifest boundaries. `test_creative_asset_inventory_evidence_binding.py` builds candidate-bound creative inventory and verifies release-index extraction accepts its candidate binding and rejects a different requested candidate. `test_candidate_evidence_layers_contract.py` is a source/document contract suite checking required release-evidence files/docs, removed temporary migrations, path/JSON hardening markers, extended performance diagnostics and optional Perfetto, cross-layer candidate/certificate hash fields, detailed human/governance review dimensions, readiness revalidation, and paired release-index/document wiring. `test_scenario_source_contract.py` pins Kotlin-reconstructed scenario and trace hashes, optional scenario flags/variants, Kotlin Float32 and round-to-long edge semantics, invalid negative/overflow literals, selective hash invalidation when scenario versus input script changes, fail-closed parser behavior, authored wolf/eagle step ownership, and GameView ordering of scripted inputs before player physics and collision. Four Python test modules read fully; not run.

`test_scenario_trace_zero_action.py` proves a scenario with no authored deterministic input script is rejected even when its scenario and trace-contract hashes are otherwise canonical. `test_validate_manifest_scenario_traces.py` exercises candidate/artifact/contract binding for referenced traces, optional absence versus required presence, valid manifest summary details, and CLI failure. `test_validate_scenario_trace.py` exercises exact root/event schemas, candidate/artifact/scenario and source-hash checks, sequence/time/dispatch/lateness/action constraints, exact authored event correspondence, duplicate-key and byte-bound rejection, and CLI expected-SHA failure. `test_audit_runtime_asset_references.py` covers exact declared/package inventory, missing/orphan/symlink assets, unexpected byte-identical groups, allowed aliases that may later diverge, and an alias plus third duplicate. `test_compare_visual_regression.py` tests identical manifests, tolerance boundaries for localized and global pixel changes, dimensions, non-PNG and symlink rejection, invalid thresholds, and CLI valid/regression/invalid statuses plus atomic JSON outputs. These five modules were read fully; not run.

`test_build_creative_asset_inventory.py` checks repository completeness while explicitly keeping all assets provenance/license-unverified, deterministic candidate-bound inventory hashes, candidate validation, per-file digest sensitivity, and symlink rejection. `test_build_declared_dependency_inventory.py` uses small Gradle/wrapper/Python fixtures to check deterministic scope-limited direct dependency records, candidate hash validation, exact Python pinning, source digest sensitivity and canonical/symlink-safe publication. `test_build_release_evidence_index.py` covers sorted candidate-bound evidence hashes, baseline separation, missing/mismatched explicit candidate binding, duplicate kind/path/hardlink/output aliases, unsafe/unsupported/missing/empty/symlink paths, strict JSON and byte limits, nonblocking/nofollow opens, FIFO replacement, canonical atomic publication and CLI no-partial-output behavior. `test_build_resolved_dependency_sbom.py` checks selected final Maven versions, coordinate deduplication, candidate-stable CycloneDX 1.6 identities, multi-report union/source hashes, conflicting-version and malformed-input rejection, symlink safety and canonical publication. `test_build_stable_release_evidence_index.py` checks independent verification, symlink-parent/FIFO rejection, nonblocking evidence opens, mutation and hardlink detection, strict duplicate-key rejection, and output removal when independent verification fails. Five modules read fully; not run.

`test_evaluate_input_latency_profiles.py` covers strict schema version typing, a passing profile, independent enforcement of all latency/sample/drop limits, measurement-kind and sample-accounting validity, percentile/total consistency, exact-over-wildcard profile precedence, ambiguity rejection, strict threshold JSON, and physical refresh-rate bounds. `test_evaluate_performance_profiles.py` validates threshold schema, pass/fail reporting across frame/heap/slow-frame and optional ghost persistence limits, malformed internal counters/ratios/timing/workload, zero refresh, missing current metrics, profile precedence/ambiguity, duplicate/nonstandard/oversized JSON and required threshold fields. `test_generate_store_assets.py` replaces only rendering/source probes to test exact candidate-bound output set and cleanup, preservation of an existing accepted directory when generation fails, and rejection of malformed candidate before publication. `test_capture_store_screenshots_contract.py` inspects shell/Python source contracts for Bash syntax, exact-main checks bracketing a fresh debug APK build, install/clear before eight deterministic readiness-gated captures, canonical device/package/activity/count, sidecar/session identity and rejection of legacy/prebuilt paths. `test_performance_collection_contract.py` checks Bash syntax, candidate identity before/after physical capture, threshold hashing, comprehensive before/after health and app diagnostics, optional but fail-closed Perfetto capture, and retention of diagnostics before reporting instrumentation failure. Five test modules read fully; not run.

`test_installed_candidate_identity.py` builds raw ADB/APK fixtures and checks package/version/installer/signer binding, the explicit limitation that installer identity proves no track, per-split signer and size/hash consistency, raw-evidence digest and symlink rejection, duplicate-key/manifest-link rejection, plus valid and malformed parser outputs. `test_installed_identity_matrix.py` composes physical acceptance and five installed-device records, checking full physical-session coverage, device/session and candidate/version/certificate consistency, distinct device serials, 24-hour temporal bound, source digests/path symlinks, and draft-preserving final compilation/revalidation. `test_play_delivery_evidence.py` builds on that matrix and checks internal-track-only delivery assertions, candidate/artifact/version/matrix binding, monotonic Play timestamps/reviewer-after-update, complete unique immutable external evidence, symlink/reviewer separation, and final manifest revalidation. `test_publish_aggregate_source_binding.py` rejects schema-valid staged aggregate mutations to candidate version, physical IDs or baseline deltas, then verifies exact reconstruction enables publication. `test_publish_device_acceptance_aggregate.py` exercises atomic candidate/baseline publication and adversarial mutation between every validation/snapshot stage, candidate/baseline substitution, output hardlink/symlink/cross-directory aliases, and forensic retention of staged data on CLI failure. Five modules read fully; not run.

`test_release_artifact_verifier.py` uses ZIP fixtures and a fake external-command runner to test required AAB config/manifest/DEX structure, duplicate/unsafe/symlink/empty entries, apkanalyzer application/version identity, complete signature and expected upload certificate checks, unsigned-entry warning rejection, explicit unsigned dry-run behavior and wrong-certificate rejection. `test_release_evidence_index_output_safety.py` exercises builder and publisher defenses against hardlink aliases, output-parent symlinks and lexical root escape, including path rechecks at publication. `test_release_evidence_index_verifier_contract.py` ensures verifier independence from the builder, strict JSON use and mutation/alias detection, builder output path/inode separation, and documented independent verification requirements. `test_verify_release_summary.py` builds machine/human release summaries and checks signed versus explicit unsigned/skip-build distinctions, candidate/screenshot/artifact freshness, content rehashes and canonical paths, required audio/graphics/metadata/R8 facts, strict JSON, numeric-vs-boolean typing, symlinks and parity with the preparer's audio catalogue. `test_verify_release_source_assets.py` synthesizes structurally valid PNG/TTF/Ogg/WAV/MP3/M4A fixtures and exercises their parsers plus CRC/geometry/table/codec/EOS failures, duplicate/unsafe asset names and audio stems, runtime catalogue parity, checked-in asset counts, release preflight order and four-frame wolf contract. `test_android_validation_action_versions.py` pins maintained checkout/Java/Gradle action versions, SDK setup ownership, cache-provider use, read-only exact-SHA checkout and explicit Android/emulator versions. `test_android_validation_workflow_contract.py` checks main/manual-only triggers, read-only exact SHA, unit/script/source-asset checks, Android Kotlin compilation/test/lint/package/release tasks, connected API 35 emulator settings, branch concurrency and job timeouts. `test_android_validation_workflow.py` independently pins main/manual-only validation, credentials-free read-only checkout, hardened connected runner and the full host release task set. `test_connected_validation_runner.py` checks runner syntax/readiness bounds/provider probes, CLI candidate flags, clean worktree and local/origin identity both sides of execution, preserved Gradle failures, emulator exclusion limited to physical profiles, serial targeting and diagnostics. `test_prepare_main_release_contract.py` checks Java path setup, candidate-bound preflight ordering, summary backup/rollback, post-build summary validation, canonical operator entrypoint and release docs free of stale invocation/cardinality/local-path rules. `test_prepare_main_release_provenance_contract.py` requires strict asset provenance approval before graphics/metadata/build and ensures provenance failures cannot be softened while the gate stays origin/candidate bound. Six modules read fully; not run.

`test_validate_human_acceptance.py` composes compiler/device fixtures and checks required class coverage, complete gameplay/accessibility/presentation check groups, constrained N/A and mandatory cutout checks, matching actual device session, linked-manifest and human-evidence digests, unique evidence path/hardlink/symlink defenses, two distinct final reviewers who authored reviews, and draft-preserving publication/revalidation. `test_validate_release_governance.py` constructs human/device/matrix/Play layers and checks all 20 decision domains and required evidence, hard approval/privacy/private-reporting gates, exact candidate links, evidence digests/path and hardlink uniqueness, release-note identity, accountable distinct final reviewers/timing, and compile publication/revalidation. `test_validate_release_readiness.py` constructs a full cross-bound evidence set and verifies success summary identity/counts; it mutates candidate, required bound index kinds, metadata text, signed artifact digest, indexed-manifest path, governance status and symlinked manifest components to ensure every independent authority is revalidated. Three modules read fully; not run.

`test_aggregate_device_acceptance_aliases.py` verifies output protection against manifest/artifact/evidence overwrite and hardlink aliases, candidate/baseline identity separation, and allowed unrelated output. `test_aggregate_device_acceptance_bundle_contract.py` checks shell preflight/trace-validation/aggregation/output-verification ordering, mandatory traces, usage errors and output collision rejection. `test_aggregate_device_acceptance.py` exercises per-class/global metrics, headroom and trace summary, baseline deltas without invented tolerance, mandatory trace presence and same trace contract sets, evidence tampering and digest-matched forged event content, matrix mismatch, finite atomic CLI output and invalid-input behavior. `test_aggregate_device_matrix.py` pins Unicode/case-normalized physical/profile identity, separates stable device identity from configuration profile changes, checks identity-field sensitivity, rejects baseline device/profile substitution and session-count mismatch, and verifies deterministic matrix hashing. `test_aggregation_trace_core_contract.py` source-checks mandatory trace validation and aggregate inclusion, hardware/trace matrix equality, stable manifest snapshot/source protection, and adversarial traced fixture support. Five modules read fully; not run.

`test_compile_device_acceptance_bounds.py` verifies draft, candidate artifact and scenario evidence upper bounds are rejected before parse/hash work, while normal bounded JSON remains accepted. `test_compile_device_acceptance_bundle_contract.py` checks wrapper syntax, strict draft preflight before compilation, strict manifest/summary postflight, independent device and mandatory-trace validation, and optional timestamp forwarding. `test_compile_device_acceptance.py` builds five-class/seven-scenario candidate fixtures and checks derived hashes without rewriting captured delivery/build identity, missing files and unsafe paths, policy/metric/build mismatch rejection, required captured build data, transactional manifest+summary publication/rollback and distinct same-directory outputs. `test_device_acceptance_hardening.py` checks policy floors for mandatory classes/scenarios, one physical device cannot count twice, Unicode/case-insensitive reviewer distinction, narrow N/A, exact manual/approval keys, candidate artifact/evidence and evidence inode uniqueness, manifest byte ceiling, and validator non-mutation. `test_validate_device_acceptance.py` defines the shared canonical acceptance fixtures and exercises valid summary, repo/branch/app/signing/store identity, device semantic/coverage requirements, scenario/evidence/build binding, performance thresholds, timing and approval constraints, CLI evidence verification/summary publication, strict duplicate-key handling, symlink manifests and path components, and separate upload versus Play app-signing certificate identities. Five modules read fully; not run.

`test_strict_json.py` covers valid JSON objects/arrays, duplicate-key and non-finite rejection, BOM/UTF-8/empty/byte/depth/root-type boundaries, missing/nonregular/FIFO files, no-follow/nonblocking descriptor use, plus recursive sorted/deduplicated evidence preflight and its empty/missing/non-JSON failures. `test_strict_json_overflow.py` covers float overflow, configurable and maximum integer digit bounds, extreme nesting and bracket characters inside strings. `test_verify_main_candidate.py` creates isolated Git repos and validates clean `main` tip acceptance, dirty/untracked/feature/detached/mismatched/abbreviated/subdirectory rejection; it also pins the canonical release wrapper's candidate freeze/recheck boundary. `test_verify_origin_main.py` creates local bare origin/work repositories and tests synchronized main, local-only commit, advanced remote, missing/wrong origin and shell syntax, then checks wrapper origin checks before/after release preparation. `test_validate_supply_chain_evidence.py` builds declared/SBOM fixtures and checks schema/candidate binding, recomputed inventory/component digests, runtime dependency inclusion, exact configurations, strict duplicate-key and symlink rejection, and lowercase full-SHA validation. `test_curate_store_screenshots.py` checks curation-manifest order/types/case-insensitive title uniqueness/scenario/digest rules, preserves accepted final output on evidence failure, and only swaps staged PNG+sidecar output after all entries validate. `test_finalize_screenshot_capture_session.py` synthesizes PNGs and sidecars, checking exact correspondence/count, candidate/origin/APK/device/package identity, unique scenarios, UTC session time after captures, and no publication on ambiguous JSON. `test_screenshot_capture_evidence.py` verifies sidecar schema/version, authored readiness/scenario/image identity, canonical package/activity/time/hash fields, same-capture identity and strict duplicate/nonfinite/size rejection. `test_verify_curated_screenshot_set.py` exercises candidate/session/sidecar/manifest linkage, exact file sets, unique scenarios/titles/hashes, CRC/chunk/trailing-byte PNG validation, landscape/metadata constraints, common capture identity/timing, and strict duplicate-key parsing. `test_verify_raw_screenshot_set.py` checks exact raw PNG/sidecar/session set, candidate/origin/device/count/time consistency, duplicate image rejection, fake PNG rejection despite matching sidecar digest, and CRC/truncation/trailing-data failures. `test_visual_baseline_provenance.py` checks provenance build/verify round-trip, strict schema/candidate SHA, baseline and manifest mutation detection, descriptor tampering, and duplicate-key rejection.
