`test_visual_regression_trusted_root.py` accepts a regular contained screenshot and rejects lexical parent traversal; `test_visual_regression_symlink_root.py` rejects a symlinked screenshot root. `test_training_control_not_applicable.py` tests live source audit completion and synthetic false-positive/positive boundaries: ordinary vocabulary, Python prose/comments, direct/dynamic/aliased framework imports, nested launcher, shell/manifest/dependency/notebook syntax, malformed notebooks, opaque archives, model artifacts, Gradle declaration extraction, source/scope digest stability, symlink findings, and required Android-tree/dependency gates. Its launcher-focused cases check stale PASS removal and truthful fail output if audit/publication fails; it defines a test that invokes the runner when the test suite is run, but this review only read the file. `test_validate_asset_provenance.py` exercises schema typing, review-required versus strict-approved coverage, first/third-party review evidence and dates, exact nonoverlapping coverage with no stale rules, unsafe/duplicate patterns/statuses, claim smuggling, symlink/strict JSON boundaries and mipmap discovery. `test_verify_android_page_size_package.py` covers candidate-bound no-native/native package assessment, strict legacy CLI mode and missing candidate, ambiguous artifact args, duplicate/unsafe ZIP paths, malformed candidate SHA, artifact/output symlinks and canonical result JSON. Five modules read fully; not run.

`test_verify_release_evidence_index.py` tests independent reconstruction of builder output, exact schema typing, evidence and set digests against disk, candidate binding, duplicate JSON keys, hardlink aliases including index/self-aliasing, symlink and FIFO rejection before unsafe open, nonblocking/nofollow descriptor flags, and mandatory candidate-bound kinds. `test_release_summary_paths.py` confirms safe Windows-style relative bundle/mapping paths normalize and pass while drive-absolute, parent-traversal, and POSIX-absolute paths fail. `test_verify_store_graphics.py` exercises candidate/source/output integrity, exact graphics dimensions and mode, complete unique manifest entries, strict schema/duplicate-key checks, stale/unmanifested assets, source/output/directory symlink rejection, and release-wrapper verification ordering. `test_verify_store_listing_parity.py` checks exact markdown-to-Play metadata projection, rejects text drift/trailing newline/duplicate headings/wrong fences, and pins parity verification before candidate metadata preparation. `test_verify_store_metadata.py` checks candidate-bound finalization and revalidation, strict exact text/manifest schema, malformed whitespace/control/placeholder rejection, duplicate entries and keys, extra files, symlinked files/manifest/directory, atomic manifest replacement, and release-wrapper ordering. All five modules were read fully; none were run.

`test_application_persistence_facade_adoption_contract.py` asserts facade ownership of run/collision/UI writes and rejects direct persistence bypasses or obsolete migration artifacts. `test_collision_query_source_contract.py` extracts all 19 encounter collision methods and prohibits gameplay/presentation/persistence side effects, with allocation-free Hedgehog and Dog checks. `test_continuation_governance_contract.py` pins durable evidence/dependency authority boundaries and accurate accessibility, security, licensing and changelog claims. `test_continuation_source_contract.py` cross-checks deterministic scenario trace/runtime schemas, physical aggregation wrappers and output protections, production spawn fairness inputs, strict JSON bounds, ghost/leitmotif admission, and overflow-safe progression scoring. `test_current_architecture_contract.py` keeps product orientation and current architecture/evidence owners synchronized across docs. `test_debug_launch_release_guard_contract.py` checks the activity and GameView two-boundary debug-only launch guard, latest-request cancellation, exact post-apply state confirmation and readiness signaling. `test_dependency_authority.py` asserts fixed plugin/library versions, no stale version catalog, centralized repositories and exact Python CI pins. `test_encounter_family_catalogue_contract.py` matches all 19 catalogue entries to entity source, factory constructor and sprite owner, ensures the catalogue does not duplicate mutable gameplay constants, and validates derived runtime profile inputs and exhaustive factory coverage. `test_familiarity_warmth_contract.py` locks pure scorer delegation, independent modifiers, restored-counter normalization, tier cutoffs and bonded-copy integration. `test_forest_journal_lifecycle_contract.py` checks selected-section Bundle restoration and recreation without progress writes; `test_forest_journal_read_only_contract.py` prohibits journal reads from materializing relationship/story/costume state and documents its observational boundary. `test_game_thread_posted_frame_contract.py` requires render completion before frame-latency completion and propagates surface posting failures. `test_game_view_audio_lifecycle_contract.py` verifies one-time manager bootstrap, state-selected cold-start music, resume/pause semantics, immediate audio-disable stop and draining transient SFX ledger. `test_game_view_frame_input_contract.py` constrains public update to lock/admission/bounded dispatch and puts mutation inside a single bounded frame owner. `test_game_view_runtime_ownership_contract.py` checks one monitor for update/render, lifecycle handoff and coherent surface transform, shared input/debug/accessibility ownership, delayed action re-entry and lock-free lifecycle joins. `test_game_view_thread_handoff_contract.py` verifies pause cancellation/quiescence, deferred latest-request restart after the prior owner stops, bounded retries, safe handling of shutdown timeout, and surface recreation/paused initialization behavior. `test_gameview_runtime_ownership_contract.py` pins shared collision effects and one session transition publication boundary while forbidding retired inline transitions and migration artifacts. `test_garden_catalogue_contract.py` cross-checks the nine ordered plant prices/names across runtime, UI and product docs; `test_garden_purchase_feedback_contract.py` centralizes success-only growth cues across touch/accessibility while keeping effects out of persistence. `test_ghost_persistence_initialization_contract.py` protects bounded executor initialization order and namespace-serial scheduling. `test_ghost_persistence_namespace_contract.py` pins one-time coherent namespace capture, immutable bound artifact stores, per-namespace publication/scheduler/pending-task bookkeeping, bounded cross-namespace concurrency, asynchronous namespace isolation and versioned codec compatibility. `test_ghost_promotion_recovery_contract.py` verifies receipt→ghost→manifest→distance persistence order, crash-recovery repair and fast paths, strong SHA-256 identity over distance and every persisted frame field, legacy identity migration, fail-closed corrupt evidence, namespace binding and matching save codec keys. `test_hardware_core_flow_capture_contract.py` requires screenshot capture to enter through the production debug-launch boundary and wait for exact screenshot mode without reflective mutation. `test_instrumentation_runtime_mutation_contract.py` enforces safe cross-thread publication and stopped-thread mutation helpers, waits for actual Bloom conversion and entity roster updates, and checks all high-risk fixture mutations are quiesced. `test_instrumentation_state_reset_contract.py` validates canonical save namespaces, reset of each recovery journal, return to primary namespace before preload, feedback/ghost cleanup and fail-closed async persistence quiescence. `test_internal_persistence_injection_visibility_contract.py` keeps injected persistence ports and constructors internal and ensures migration scaffolding is absent. `test_leitmotif_throttle_contract.py` pins dedicated evaluation throttles as the only parameter update gate. `test_live_accessibility_provider_contract.py` checks the direct virtual-node host, truthful live semantics, real-owner actions, stance guards and release gates, selective node invalidation, coalesced touch-exploration announcements, and absence of synthetic touch dispatch or retired delegate. `test_live_runtime_integration_contract.py` verifies GameView routes every collision outcome through an exhaustive dispatcher whose branch input factories stay lazy. `test_main_menu_input_contract.py` requires finite coordinate admission before feedback, garden callbacks or phase dispatch and validates its balanced source extractor. `test_nonterminal_collision_outcome_contract.py` pins STUMBLE/MERCY_MISS lazy inputs, complete outcome identity, no inline effects in GameView, ordered coordinator side effects and shared live effect adapter ownership. `test_parallax_bloom_admission_contract.py` checks NaN/infinity closed admission and one normalized finite [0,1] boundary across state, profile and draw paths. `test_parallax_frame_input_contract.py` requires raw frame/speed validation first and all subsequent parallax mutation to use bounded inputs. `test_player_animation_ownership_contract.py` pins explicit jump-strip frame partitions, the Willow stand-up ritual and dedicated stumble/rest sequences. `test_prepare_play_release_graphics_manifest.py` tests strict bounded graphics-manifest parsing and exact unique required output pair. `test_privacy_contract.py` cross-checks manifest permissions/backup policy, production SDK/network dependency and import exclusions, and corresponding privacy policy claims. `test_recovery_evidence_maintenance_contract.py` verifies exact domain handler coverage, safe repair versus confirmed destructive discard, privacy-limited status summaries, one captured namespace, bound persistence sinks/artifact owners, and post-clear reinspection. `test_recovery_evidence_user_ui_contract.py` checks one-time activity dialog lifecycle, facade-based privacy-safe presentation and explicitly confirmed destructive actions. `test_recovery_maintenance_launch_contract.py` validates debug-only maintenance commands, startup ordering after save repair but before game construction, inspection-only reused activities, cold-start mutation constraints, one-shot intents, status-only logs and exact action/domain parsing. `test_relationship_familiarity_scoring_contract.py` keeps relationship-stage ownership delegating all score inputs and tier thresholds to the canonical pure scorer. `test_repository_asset_provenance_contract.py` checks complete one-rule asset inventory and release blocking until actual approval. `test_return_moment_arithmetic_contract.py` protects saturating streak arithmetic, rollback-safe 36-hour absence detection, invalid-order guards and shared bounds. `test_route_counter_ceiling_parity.py` keeps normal persistence, recovery journal and atomic snapshots aligned on the derived counter ceiling. `test_run_outcome_persistence_contract.py` validates exactly-once terminal commit, impact-before-capture and typed session handoff, reset/recovery gates, durable-before-ghost recovery record, staged progression/snapshot ordering and fail-closed recovery. `test_run_outcome_recovery_record_validation_contract.py` pins required summary serialization keys, canonical after-state transition checks, SaveManager bounds and validation before pending state exposure. `test_run_outcome_recovery_store_contract.py` checks namespace-scoped schema-v2 journal, synchronous writes, complete summary and before/after snapshots, saturated mood/return/route transitions and one-commit summary snapshots. `test_run_session_state_ownership_contract.py` enforces the single GameView state publication boundary and side-effect-free explicit debug event. `test_screenshot_capture_script_contract.py` checks origin/main and candidate identity around fresh build, foreground activity before and after capture, strict sidecar writer use, exact command count and session finalization; its test contains a Bash syntax subprocess, which was read but not executed here. `test_species_sfx_contract.py` pins Eagle/Owl one-shot threat cues and Dog/Wolf-specific calls behind sample/audio readiness. `test_supply_chain_purl_binding.py` verifies that SBOM builder and evidence validator use the same canonical Maven purl encoding and reject rehashed noncanonical or field-mismatched component identities. `test_supply_chain_workflow_contract.py` pins the two exact runtime classpath reports and checked-in candidate-bound builders/validator while excluding obsolete Gradle SBOM tasks. `test_terminal_hit_impact_contract.py` protects immediate impact side-effect order, impact-before-snapshot capture, identity consistency and typed death handoff. `test_terminal_hit_outcome_contract.py` checks post-impact capture, completion and session order, frame exit before progress mutation, known-killer gating, one-time summary/persistence and adapter ownership. `test_validate_device_acceptance_aggregate.py` builds canonical candidate/baseline reports and rejects schema extras, forged weighted metrics/matrix, identifier/count/class drift, impossible trace counts, negative headroom, baseline binding/semantics changes and duplicate JSON; it also binds to the actual producer shape. `test_write_screenshot_capture_evidence.py` builds PNG fixtures and validates candidate-bound loadable sidecars, atomic replacement after successful validation, CRC rejection without sidecar publication and strict capture identity/readiness inputs. `MainActivityRobolectricTest` smoke-checks Activity content initialization and reflection-verifies a replacement debug launch invalidates the prior latest-request token, while destroy cancels ownership. `DeterministicScenarioPartitionInvariantTest` checks every scripted scenario dispatches the exact authored action/time sequence under coarse, fine, irregular and backward/nonfinite frame observations; rejected trace events do not consume valid sequence numbers. `DeterministicScenarioReplayContractTest` accepts complete authored replay, rejects unscripted scenarios and rejects missing/extra/rescheduled/substituted events. `DeterministicScenarioTraceEvidenceStoreTest` checks deterministic scenario filenames, replacement of complete payload, digest validation before writes and non-directory rejection. `DeterministicScenarioTraceEvidenceTest` validates default trace retention after clear, canonical deterministic schema-v2 evidence fields and hashes, and fail-closed malformed/incomplete/overflowed/unbound input. `EntityOutcomeIntegrationTest` composes real EntityManager, all entity types, persistence and deterministic Orb control to verify arbitration priority, pending encounter bounds, exactly-once clean/spare accounting, malformed geometry fail-closed behavior, exclusive Bloom conversion, optional Orb corridor safety, swept contact severity and nonpersistent debug isolation. `RecoveryEvidenceMaintenanceIntegrationTest` uses actual preference and atomic-file evidence owners to check clean/pending/corrupt inspection, safe recovery, targeted confirmed discard, and ghost receipt/manifest/distance repairs while preserving durable ghost artifacts. `RecoveryEvidenceUserControllerDispositionTest` covers failed/unavailable/recovered-instead/discarded action mapping and reinspection of independent domains. `RecoveryEvidenceUserControllerTest` checks state-specific action presentation, private-detail redaction, explicit destructive confirmation, safe retry routing, domain isolation and exception-safe model retention. Nine test files were read fully; none were executed.

The entity/player test cluster now also covers `CostumeOverlayTest` (bounded animation time, malformed geometry/motion and transparent rejection), `EntityCollisionGeometryTest` (strict overlap, symmetric/axis/edge padding, extreme finite expansion and malformed rectangles), `EntityFactoryTest` (finite defaults for every type with malformed geometry and valid-origin preservation), and `FaceManagerTest` (blink timing recovery, invalid body rejection and finite-motion fallback). `PlayerBloomIntegrationTest` confirms invulnerability layers over ascent/fall/landing without replacing locomotion; `PlayerBoundaryTest` checks input admission, stance state, invalid/capped frame deltas, poisoned kinematics repair, Bloom bounds, foot-anchored hitboxes and correct pre-frame sweep sample. `PlayerJumpVelocityTest` and `PlayerPhysicsTest` test bounded monotonic hold-to-jump interpolation and tap/full-hold endpoints. `PlayerSecondaryMotionTest` covers locomotion/Bloom transforms, malformed and extreme inputs, deterministic bounds, and rest-state behavior. `SwayCollisionAlignmentTest` proves visual sway leaves Cactus, Orchid, Willow and Cherry collision geometry rooted. These ten test files were read fully; none were executed.

Five animal encounter tests were read: `CatTest` and `FoxTest` compare familiar-history reward/aura to baseline; `DogTest` covers bonded buddy reward, compact max-speed escort cull ordering, projectile body-hit precedence and synchronized swept body/projectile contacts; `HedgehogTest` covers telegraph timing at max speed, nonlethal direct body collision, provisional outer mercy band, safe-departure resolution and stronger warned-pass reward; `WolfTest` checks repeated-spare stand-down and reward. Five bird encounter tests were read: `ChickadeeGroupTest` checks full-body flutter-pocket readability, empty fallback when no safe lane exists, aggregate bounds and per-bird collision arbitration; `DuckTest` validates call/low-lane answer and actual crouch traversal across screen heights; `EagleTest` checks moving telegraph/held-mark rewards and authored staging offsets; `OwlTest` verifies persistent-history flags and viewport-gated one-time alert; `TitGroupTest` covers trough threading with real jump trajectory, reward only on complete fit, aggregate bounds and per-bird hit/near-miss arbitration. All ten tests were read fully; none were executed.

The flora/tree test cluster covers five flora types and five tree tests. `CactusTest`, `LilyOfValleyTest`, and `EucalyptusTest` check component hit/mercy/clearance and stable scroll geometry. `HyacinthTest` distinguishes thorn core, soft brush and outer mercy, tests synchronized swept mid-frame stumble at capped speed, terminal stumble without later pass, and delays pass credit until complete brush departure. `VanillaOrchidTest` preserves the open thread between separate lower/upper hitboxes, validates swept upper-band collision and proves actual jumping-player fit across density buckets. `BambooGapPlacementTest` validates seeded opening range against sampled jump-body trajectories, complete multi-stalk exposure time, actual uninterrupted crossing, per-stalk swept collision, impossible landscape rejection and spawn-pool omission. `BambooTest` checks the featured seam and aggregate bounds over both sway directions. `CherryBlossomTest` separates branch, storm-veil and trunk risk, checks maximum-step trunk tunneling and incomplete visual bounds. `JacarandaTest` validates the undershoot safe lane versus solid branch/trunk and real duck passage at reference heights and speeds. `WeepingWillowTest` similarly validates the curtain/trunk collision contract and real duck lane passage through the complete moving tree. All ten files were read fully; none were executed.

Audit continuation (2026-10-03): hardened curated screenshot evidence reads with no-follow regular-file descriptors, bounded reads, stable inode/size/timestamp checks, and symlink-free screenshot directory admission. Capture sidecar semantic validation is now reusable for already-parsed strict JSON. Added a regression for a curated PNG symlink whose bytes exactly match the expected screenshot. The curated screenshot verifier suite passed 17 tests, capture-evidence suite passed 7 tests, and the complete repository Python script suite passed 857 tests. `JAVA_HOME=/opt/android-studio/jbr ./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` reported BUILD SUCCESSFUL (Gradle marked the test task UP-TO-DATE, so this was not a fresh JVM test execution). `git diff --check` passed. Live curated screenshot capture remains unverified: the expected capture-session evidence/final screenshot set is absent. Connected Android tests were not run in this continuation because `adb` is not on PATH. Earlier inventory sections describe static reading and previous execution snapshots; they are not claims that every live workflow or device path has passed.

Verification continuation (2026-10-03): resolved the earlier Android test access issue by using the SDK-local `platform-tools/adb`, booted `Medium_Phone_API_36.1` (API 36) with 2 GiB guest RAM and mounted its initially unmounted public volume. A fresh `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest --rerun-tasks` executed all tasks and succeeded. `connectedDebugAndroidTest` completed all 30 instrumented tests with 0 failures and 0 skips in 4m44s; XML report: `app/build/outputs/androidTest-results/connected/debug/TEST-Medium_Phone_API_36.1(AVD) - 16-_app-.xml`. `lintDebug` and `lintRelease` passed with 84 warnings each and no Fatal/Error issues. `assembleDebug` and `assembleRelease` passed; release R8 minification and resource optimization executed. `git diff --check` passed. Updated `ISSUE_NOTES.md` after finding its old reports pointed to a different checkout; each was checked against this repo and marked historical/resolved/not present. Physical-device acceptance and actual curated screenshot evidence remain open external gates.

PNG parser continuation (2026-10-03): further audit found curated screenshots were accepted without validating PNG scanline filter bytes, Adam7 decoded layout, or indexed-pixel references into PLTE. The verifier now checks filter values, exact decoded row sizes across standard scanlines or all seven Adam7 passes, reverses filters for indexed images, and validates every palette index against the declared palette while enforcing the decoded-byte ceiling. Added regressions for invalid filter type, valid Adam7 layout, and missing indexed palette entries. Pillow-generated in-memory PNGs in modes 1, L, LA, RGB, RGBA, P, and I;16 all passed the parser probe (Pillow emitted non-interlaced files despite the interlace request). The live verifier currently fails closed because `release/google-play/screenshots/raw/` is absent; this verifies that the capture gate is enforced, not that screenshot evidence has been produced or reviewed.

Connected-test correction (2026-10-03): audit of the XML report showed the earlier plain `connectedDebugAndroidTest` 30-test run included all `@LargeTest` performance and screenshot-capture harnesses on a SwiftShader emulator; it does not constitute physical-device evidence. `docs/RELEASE.md` now documents the ordinary-run `notAnnotation=androidx.test.filters.LargeTest` argument. The first filtered attempt was cut off at test 10/20 when the host OOM killer terminated qemu during `biomeCycleTransitionsAcrossLongRunOnDevice`; the kernel recorded qemu at 3.57 GiB RSS and the emulator serial vanished. After stopping the idle Gradle 8.13 daemon, restarting the emulator with two cores, and running with a 768 MiB single-use Gradle daemon, the filtered suite completed 20/20 with 0 failures/errors/skips in 1m04s. XML report contains no Hardware* test classes. `test_connected_validation_runner` and `test_android_validation_workflow` passed 16 checks. Physical-device evidence remains uncollected.

PNG filter algorithm round-trip (2026-10-03): added alternating 1-bit indexed rows encoded with each PNG filter type 0–4. Pillow fully decoded all five generated images, and the curated-set verifier accepted the candidate-bound sets. Screenshot verifier suite passes 21 tests and the entire Python script suite passes 861 tests. `git diff --check` passes. The pinned project requirement is Pillow 11.3.0; local Pillow 12.1.1 reports `Image.getdata()` deprecated, but `get_flattened_data()` was added only in 12.1, so changing to it without changing the project pin would break the declared dependency floor.

Capture publication hardening (2026-10-03): source review found `capture_store_screenshots.sh` deleted previously accepted raw evidence before device validation/capture and wrote new PNGs directly to the published directory. A managed-name symlink could survive its `find -type f` cleanup and be followed by screenshot redirection. The script now captures into a sibling staging directory, removes all managed names regardless of file type, finalizes and verifies the full raw set, rechecks candidate and origin, then calls an atomic directory publisher with rollback. The publisher rejects symlinked output paths/ancestors and cross-parent renames, restores the prior set if publication fails, and preserves unrelated prior output entries. `finalize_screenshot_capture_session.py` now rejects symlinked output directory paths before writing session evidence. Added publisher rollback/preservation/symlink tests, finalizer symlink test, and capture ordering/symlink cleanup contract checks. Full Python suite passes 870 tests; capture/finalizer/publisher focused coverage is 10+7+6 tests, Bash syntax and `git diff --check` pass. The end-to-end capture script was not run because this checkout is modified and its production path requires a clean canonical `main` candidate; the live `raw/` capture directory remains absent.

Transactional release compiler rollback recovery (2026-10-03): failure-path review found all three JSON evidence compilers could delete transaction directories even when restoring an earlier destination failed, destroying the only remaining backup. The Play delivery, release governance, and human acceptance publishers now retain their transaction directory and report its path whenever rollback has any error. Fault-injection tests force the second publication and first backup restoration to fail, then verify the earlier destination is restored and the unrecovered backup remains available for manual recovery. The three focused suites pass 9, 13, and 11 tests; the full Python script suite passes 874 tests. The suite emits expected negative-case diagnostics and a Pillow deprecation warning; it exits successfully. This does not claim recovery from power loss or filesystem/device failure outside the tested rename/unlink error paths.

Evidence compiler output aliasing (2026-10-03): the three evidence `compile_file` functions resolved output symlinks before checking destination constraints. A symlink to another file in the draft directory therefore passed validation and could replace that file. Each public compiler entrypoint and low-level publisher now rejects symlink destinations before resolution. Regression tests assert rejection and byte preservation of the symlink target for Play delivery, release governance, and human acceptance. The focused suites pass 10, 14, and 12 tests; the complete Python suite passes 877 tests. Python compile checks and `git diff --check` pass. Parent-component race attacks and power-loss durability are outside these tests.

Evidence compiler chain extension (2026-10-03): scanning every `scripts/compile_*.py` publisher found the same output-symlink redirect in installed identity matrix and device acceptance. Installed identity matrix additionally deleted its transaction directory after failed rollback; device acceptance reported rollback errors but still removed that directory. Both now reject output symlinks at public and publisher boundaries and retain backups when rollback reports any error. Fault-injection tests verify rollback backup preservation; output-symlink regressions verify targets remain byte-identical. Focused installed-matrix and device-acceptance suites pass 10 tests each; the full Python script suite passes 881 tests. Python compile checks and `git diff --check` pass. The current source scan found no sixth `compile_*.py` publisher, but does not establish crash consistency under power loss or hostile concurrent parent replacement.

Release evidence directory rollback continuation (2026-10-03): the wider publisher scan found screenshot curation deleted `.final-backup` before every retry, so an interrupted prior promotion could lose the previous accepted screenshot set. A retry now restores a backup when the final directory is absent and refuses ambiguous coexistence without deleting either set. Fault injection verifies a failed retry leaves the old accepted files restored. `prepare_main_release.sh` had a related gap: its exit trap deleted the summary quarantine even when `mv` restoration failed. It now reports and retains the transaction directory on restore/cleanup failure, removes failed generated summaries, and a shell-level regression simulates a failed restore. Focused curation tests pass 8, release-wrapper contract tests pass 10, Bash syntax passes, and the complete Python suite passes 885 tests. This still does not prove power-loss durability or concurrent directory mutation safety.

Graphics directory publication retry (2026-10-03): `generate_store_assets.publish_directory` also removed its fixed backup before retrying, losing the previously accepted graphics if an earlier process stopped after moving output to backup. It now recovers backup when output is absent and refuses to choose between coexisting output and backup directories. The public entrypoint rejects output symlinks before resolving paths. Fault-injection tests cover restoration after a retry publication error, coexistence preservation, and output symlink refusal. The focused generator suite passes 6 tests; the entire Python suite passes 888 tests. `git diff --check` and Python compilation pass. Power-loss durability and concurrent parent replacement remain outside this evidence.

Directory publisher path-type validation (2026-10-03): screenshot curation and graphics generation treated any existing final/backup/staging path as if it were a directory. A regular file at a managed directory name could be moved into a backup and deleted after a successful publish. Both publishers now reject non-directory managed paths before moving or deleting them; symlink checks remain in place. Regression tests assert malformed output/backup paths and their bytes remain intact. Focused suites pass 9 and 7 tests; the full Python suite passes 890 tests. This is path-type validation, not a guarantee against concurrent replacement after validation.

Fresh JVM/Robolectric verification (2026-10-03): reran `JAVA_HOME=/opt/android-studio/jbr ./gradlew testDebugUnitTest --rerun-tasks`; all 30 actionable tasks executed and Gradle reported `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` in 1m09s. Summed the 241 XML test reports: 1,288 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped. Kotlin compilation emitted existing deprecation/unused-value warnings; no test failure was reported. The complete Python suite on this worktree separately passes 890 tests.

Coverage-driven raw screenshot input hardening (2026-10-03): measuring Python line/branch coverage showed the raw curation image path was not exercised by existing tests. An explicit symlink probe reproduced that `inspect_image()` followed a screenshot symlink outside the raw directory. It now rejects symlinked/non-regular files and symlinked parents, reads at most the configured 50 MiB from a no-follow descriptor, verifies inode/size/time identity before and after the read, and hashes/decodes the same in-memory bytes. New tests verify real PNG digest/dimensions and file/parent symlink rejection. Added a full production store-graphics render test, raising that module's measured line coverage from 44% to 81%; curated screenshot curation is at 77% and screenshot publication at 80%. The complete Python suite passes 897 tests. This improves exercised scope but does not claim exhaustive branch coverage for all Python or Kotlin code.

Screenshot image-gate coverage continuation (2026-10-03): added adversarial tests for missing, empty, oversized, non-regular and corrupt files; minimum/maximum dimensions; portrait orientation; blank content; black edge bands and the explicit override; and same-inode mutation during descriptor reading. A fixture initially rotated without expanding and correctly remained landscape; after fixing the fixture, all 14 focused curator tests and all 900 Python script tests pass. A fresh branch-coverage run reports line coverage of 82% for screenshot curation, 81% for graphics generation, and 80% for screenshot set publication. Across those three modules combined, 82% statement coverage remains incomplete and the whole scripts tree is lower; coverage guides further work and is not treated as proof of exhaustive correctness.

Legacy final-asset importer coverage and grid correction (2026-10-03): coverage found `import_final_assets.py` had no automated execution. Running its 31 mappings into a temporary output exposed a grid slicing mismatch: 640px sheets divided into six or three rows lost the bottom remainder and used incorrect equal-truncated boundaries. The slicer now validates mapping bounds and assigns pixels using proportional integer cell edges, so all source pixels belong to a frame. Added synthetic tests across quantization, edge flood fill, connected components, crop, support removal, character background, packing, import, and malformed mapping values. The full 31-output run into temporary storage succeeds and all outputs contain visible pixels. The new unit module covers 93% of importer statements; the complete script suite passes 906 tests. This importer still writes directly to its configured destination when run normally; the audit run redirected it to temporary storage and did not modify shipped assets.

Curation validate-to-copy identity (2026-10-03): review found the curator reopened raw PNG and sidecar paths with `shutil.copy2()` after validation. A mutation between validation and staging could publish bytes that were not bound by the verified hashes/sidecar. Curation now captures stable bounded raw and sidecar bytes, rechecks parsed sidecar identity against the validated record, and writes those exact byte strings into staging. Fault injection mutates both source files at staging time and confirms final output remains the bytes previously validated; another injection changes the sidecar between parser passes and confirms publication aborts while the prior final set remains intact. The 16 focused curator tests and all 908 Python tests pass. Current coverage is 83% for screenshot curation and 93% for the legacy importer. Earlier real-source import integration remains temporary-only and passed all 31 outputs.

Play release preparation parser regression (2026-10-03): release identity review found `parse_assignment()` could accept a commented-out `targetSdk` before the active Gradle assignment, causing the static gate to validate the wrong SDK value. It now masks Kotlin line and nested block comments while preserving newlines, then accepts assignments only at line starts; comment markers inside strings are preserved by the scanner. Regression tests cover line comments, nested block comments, and the project identity gate with a commented stale SDK before an invalid active SDK. The focused preparer tests pass 8/8, the full Python suite passes 916 tests, and a fresh `JAVA_HOME=/opt/android-studio/jbr ./gradlew testDebugUnitTest --rerun-tasks` run passes 1,288 tests with zero failures/errors/skips. Python compilation and `git diff --check` pass. This is static Gradle text validation; it does not replace Android Gradle Plugin evaluation of arbitrary Kotlin DSL expressions.

Candidate identity collection publication and collector branch audit (2026-10-03): source review found installed identity collection created its destination and raw evidence before checking for SDK tools or an authorized device. Failed attempts left partial output that prevented retries. Collection now stages the whole bundle in a private sibling directory, validates the staged manifest independently, and publishes it with one rename; failures remove staging, refuse output symlinks, and preserve destinations changed concurrently. Further coverage review found installer output matching package ID prefixes, so a `.debug` package row could make the canonical release package appear ambiguous. Installer parsing now matches the exact package token. SDK command-line tool resolution now prefers `cmdline-tools/latest`, or otherwise the highest numeric version, instead of depending on directory iteration order. Twenty-one collector tests cover parsing boundaries, SDK discovery, exact package collision handling, invalid device/package/APK identity, candidate drift, retry/cleanup, CLI entry, and a two-split mocked collection accepted by the independent evidence validator. The no-trainable-surface wrapper tests cover complete/incomplete audit reports and refusal of symlinked output paths; the real repository audit passes with 659 source/config files scanned, zero findings, and no training-execution claim. The full Python suite passes 941 tests; branch coverage measures the collector at 98% line / 96% branch and the audit wrapper at 88% line / 71% branch. One collector line and seven branches remain uncovered, mostly the defensive duplicate-evidence invariant and SDK candidate-discovery branches. Installed-identity matrix reader and provenance hardening (2026-10-03): adversarial source review found `load_and_validate()` called `read_bytes()` before rejecting symlinks, FIFOs, and over-limit manifests, which could block on a FIFO or read an arbitrarily large payload before enforcing the configured 8 MiB limit. It now rejects type/size first and reads through a bounded no-follow, nonblocking descriptor with inode/metadata checks before and after. A second consistency flaw hashed referenced device and installed-identity manifests, then reopened them for separate validation and parsing; validation now parses and validates the exact bounded bytes whose SHA-256 is recorded. `_safe_relative()` also rejects paths that `PurePosixPath` would silently normalize (redundant separators, dot segments, trailing slash). Added FIFO/symlink/oversize, descriptor flags, path-replacement, in-memory validator, primitive helper, CLI, and path-normalization tests. The focused module passes 19 tests and reaches 82% measured line coverage. Full Python suite passes 1,001 tests; `git diff --check` and Python compilation pass. Fresh no-trainable-surface audit is complete with zero findings, output matches the stored report, and makes no execution claim. Remaining validator branches and other production modules are still under audit.


Installed-package inspection and matrix-validator continuation (2026-10-03): found that the Android package inspector opened an artifact without `O_NONBLOCK` before checking its file type; it now uses nonblocking/close-on-exec/no-follow flags and rejects a FIFO promptly. Added tests for FIFO, empty/oversized inputs, archive path and ZIP failures, entry-count/entry-size limits, descriptor flags, and positional CLI publication. Focused tests pass 22 cases; measured line coverage rose from 68% to 75%. The installed-identity matrix validator now reads its own and its referenced manifests via one bounded no-follow descriptor, validates the same in-memory bytes whose digest it records, and enforces canonical normalized relative paths. Regression tests cover oversized/symlink/FIFO inputs, replacement during reads, descriptor flags, and preventing downstream validators from reopening hashed manifests. The full Python suite passes 1,014 tests. Fresh audit is complete with zero findings across 659 files, matches its stored JSON, and emits no execution claim; Python compilation and `git diff --check` pass. Transient haptic lifecycle concurrency review (2026-10-04): `HapticManager` checked the volatile enabled preference and invoked the platform vibrator without sharing an ordering boundary with `cancel()`. A cue admitted before pause/disable could therefore issue after cancellation. Pulse, waveform, and cancellation operations now synchronize on the manager so a lifecycle cancel runs after an in-flight cue and later cues observe the disabled preference. A deterministic monitor-ordering regression test covers cue admission and cancellation. At this point, full Android debug and release JVM suites each passed 1,288 tests; the final rerun after adding the regression passes 1,289 tests in each variant with zero failures/errors/skips under JBR 21. Physical-device motor behavior remains unverified.
